# Skills · A · part 3

851 entries, aa-init to ab-test-design-evaluation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **aa-init**: Scaffold the AA/ task-board convention into the current repo — a Kanban system kept in git, in any discipline. One page of rules, a backlog/tasks/done split where the directory IS the state, an append-only checkpoint trail per run so a dead session or a killed subagent still says what was in flight, and a STATE.md board that is GENERATED from the task files and git history rather than hand-maintained (board.sh --check fails when it is stale). Flow metrics — work item age, cycle time, throughput, flow efficiency, Little's Law — are computed, never written down. One AA.yml adapts it to the disc… ([adervark/AGEAris](https://github.com/adervark/AGEAris/tree/HEAD/skills/aa-init))
- **aa-jira-update**: Soạn và đăng cập nhật trên Jira của Adventure Asia cho chị Thư (các project PR, KAN, CON; epic theo app PR-22…PR-27, báo cáo tuần PR-20) — luôn nháp trước, chờ Nghiệp duyệt rồi mới đăng; tiếng Việt, bảng số liệu, mention @thule bằng mention node, ảnh nhúng. Dùng khi cần trả lời hoặc báo tiến độ trên Jira, soạn báo cáo tuần, đọc yêu cầu mới từ Jira, hoặc đổi trạng thái issue Jira. ([AdventureAsia365-Ecosys/AA-Ecosys](https://github.com/AdventureAsia365-Ecosys/AA-Ecosys/tree/HEAD/skill/aa-jira-update))

- **aas-topic-selection**: 当你在为《自动化学报》(Acta Automatica Sinica, AAS) 确定选题、判断稿件是否契合本刊控制与自动化学科定位、以及在综述/论文/短文三类栏目间定位时调用。帮助辨析本刊与兄弟中文刊(计算机学报/软件学报/电子学报/计算机研究与发展/计算机科学)的学科分工，评估创新性与贡献可数性，避免"主题超范围""无新意""重复前人"等初审退稿理由，并把选题打磨到匹配 Acta Automatica Sinica 的控制/模式识别/机器人/智能系统侧重与中英文双语综述长文传统。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Automatica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aas-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **aas-workflow**: 当你在为《自动化学报》(Acta Automatica Sinica, AAS) 规划一次完整投稿的时间线、把 12 个技能编排成从选题到见刊的流水线时调用。给出选题定位→写作→实验→匿名投稿→双盲三审→多轮退修→录用定稿的阶段划分、每阶段的准入准出、里程碑与常见延误点，帮助作者在 Acta Automatica Sinica 的双盲三审制与中英文双语综述长文传统下合理排期，避免作者承诺漏交、双盲泄露、退修超期等踩坑，并在每个节点指明应调用哪个 aas- 技能。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Automatica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aas-workflow) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **aas-writing-style**: 当你在为《自动化学报》(Acta Automatica Sinica, AAS) 撰写或打磨中文稿件体例时调用。覆盖报道性中英文摘要(目的/方法/结果/结论四要素)、中英文关键词、中图分类号(自动化/计算机类 TP 打头)、参考文献 GB/T 7714 顺序编码制著录、量与单位法定计量、控制类公式与图表规范、以及综述/论文/短文三类栏目的体例差异，帮助把控制理论、模式识别、机器人、智能系统方向的中文长文写到符合 Acta Automatica Sinica 的双语传统与编排要求。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Automatica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aas-writing-style) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
